<input type='checkbox' name='article_1315' value='https://finance.yahoo.com/video/season-tax-strategy-invest-small-200000943.html' /> 1315 - <a href='https://www.google.com/search?q=finance.yahoo.com+This+season%E2%80%99s+tax+strategy%3A+invest+in+small+businessListen+and+subscribe+to+The+Big+Idea+with+Elizabeth+Gore+on+Apple+Podcasts%2C+Spotify%2C+or+wherever+you+find+your+favorite+podcast.+This+tax+season%2C+your+investment+could+pay+off+quite+literally+for+you+and+your+local+businesses.+In+a+special+segment+from+this+week%E2%80%99s+episode+of+The+Big+Idea+with+Elizabeth+Gore%2C+Elizabeth+is+joined+by+H%26R+Block+Small+Business+Tax+Director+Jody+Vanarsdale+to+discuss+how+investing+in+small+business+stocks+can+benefit+both+investors+and+local+entrepreneurs.+From+capital+gains+tax+relief+to+supporting+community+businesses%2C+Jody+explains+how+this+opportunity+helps+grow+cash+flow%2C+strengthen+P%26Ls%2C+and+turn+tax+season+into+a+strategic+investment+moment.+Yahoo+Finance%E2%80%99s+The+Big+Idea+with+Elizabeth+Gore+takes+you+on+a+journey+with+America%E2%80%99s+entrepreneurs+as+they+navigate+the+world+of+small+business.+This+post+was+written+by+Lauren+Pokedoff' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Search - </a> <a href='https://12ft.io/https://finance.yahoo.com/video/season-tax-strategy-invest-small-200000943.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>This season’s tax strategy: invest in small businessListen and subscribe to The Big Idea with Elizabeth Gore on Apple Podcasts, Spotify, or wherever you find your favorite podcast. This tax season, your investment could pay off quite literally for you and your local businesses. In a special segment from this week’s episode of The Big Idea with Elizabeth Gore, Elizabeth is joined by H&R Block Small Business Tax Director Jody Vanarsdale to discuss how investing in small business stocks can benefit both investors and local entrepreneurs. From capital gains tax relief to supporting community businesses, Jody explains how this opportunity helps grow cash flow, strengthen P&Ls, and turn tax season into a strategic investment moment. Yahoo Finance’s The Big Idea with Elizabeth Gore takes you on a journey with America’s entrepreneurs as they navigate the world of small business. This post was written by Lauren Pokedoff</a><br>
